BSRS 104-011 
Issue 45 
July 1, 1983 
Page 2 


1. GENERAL 

1.01 This BSRS provides an alphabetical index of available Bell System 
Repair Specifications which have been covered in BSRS Lists 365 through 
372 prepared by the Indiana Publication Center. It is the intent that 
BSRS Lists issued subsequent to List 372 are to be considered supplementary 
to this alphabetical index until it is reissued. It is the plan to 
reissue this index annually. 

1.02 Changes and additions are indicated by asterisks (*) in the 
left-hand margin. 


Western Electric 

Distribution and Repair Services

Issue 45 


July 1, 1983
